- **assembly-error-rate-assessment**: Use when comparing de novo/hybrid assemblers on identical read datasets and you need error-rate evidence (misassemblies, mismatch/indel rates) plus contiguity (NGA50, genome fraction) and resource cost (runtime, peak memory) per tool. - **assembly-error-rate-quantification**: Use when comparing de novo assembler versions or assemblies on a reference-backed benchmark and you need continuity (N50) plus per-error-class rates (structural, substitution, copy number) to claim improvement. - **assembly-error-vs-heterozygous-loci-classification**: Use when evaluating a de novo genome assembly (e.g. HiCanu contigs of a heterozygous F1 hybrid) where clipped alignment signals must be split into true misassemblies versus haplotype differences before computing assembly quality indexes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/assembly-error-vs-heterozygous-loci-classification))

- **assembly-fundamentals-and-isas**: Use when deciding whether to write assembly at all, learning or comparing instruction sets, or reasoning about what the machine actually does beneath the ISA. - **assembly-qc**: Quantify the quality, completeness, and contamination of a bacterial genome assembly AND write a pass/warn/fail report.md. ([edotheDev/ai-animation-workflow-by-dotsstudio](https://github.com/edotheDev/ai-animation-workflow-by-dotsstudio/tree/HEAD/skills/assembly-qc))

([LunCoSim/lunco-sim](https://github.com/LunCoSim/lunco-sim/tree/HEAD/skills/assembly-quality) · ★109)
- **assembly-quality-assessment**: Use when preprocessed paired-end metagenomic reads must be de novo assembled and the resulting contigs must be quality-checked via contig count, total length (bp), and N50, e.g. to reproduce VIRGO's assembly cohort statistics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/assembly-quality-assessment))
- **assembly-quality-assessment-metrics**: Use when assemblies from multiple de novo assemblers must be scored per contig against known reference replicons, requiring a Phred-like quality metric derived from alignment identity rather than raw sequence features. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/assembly-quality-assessment-metrics))
- **assembly-quality-assessment-quast**: Use when you have long-read assemblies (raw Miniasm/Flye assemblies, CONSENT-polished, or RACON-polished) plus a reference genome and need standardized metrics (NGA50, NGA75, misassemblies, genome fraction, errors per 100 kbp) to compare correction or polishing tools. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/assembly-quality-assessment-quast))
- **assembly-quality-assessment-with-quast**: Use when two shotgun read sets (e.g. a chemically treated library such as RIMS-seq and its untreated control) have each been assembled de novo and you must quantify whether treatment altered assembly quality via N50 and mismatch rate. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/assembly-quality-assessment-with-quast))
- **assembly-quality-evaluation-with-quast**: Use when FASTQ read sets (simulated or experimental nanopore) have been assembled de novo and consensus quality must be scored per genome against a ground-truth reference to judge simulator or sequencer fidelity. - **assembly-quality-index-calculation**: Use when a draft genome assembly needs a numeric regional/structural quality score (R-AQI/S-AQI) based on clipped-read error counts, or when heterozygous loci must be separated from true assembly errors. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/assembly-quality-index-calculation))
- **assembly-quality-metric-computation**: Use when assemblies have been produced at multiple long-read coverage levels (e.g., 5x-45x PacBio) and you need per-level NGA50 and genome fraction values, aligned to a reference genome, to identify coverage plateaus. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/assembly-quality-metric-correlation-analysis))
- **assembly-quality-overcorrection-assessment**: Use when a polished HiFi/T2T assembly must be validated against its draft: you need to confirm QV gains, reduction of homozygous high-quality variants, and absence of overcorrection or switch errors before accepting the polished consensus. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/assembly-quality-overcorrection-assessment))

- **assembly-quality-score-thresholding**: Use when PANDAseq has assembled Illumina overlapping paired-end (e.g. 16S rRNA amplicon) reads and you must choose how many low-confidence assemblies to retain or discard via the assembly quality score cutoff. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/assembly-quality-score-thresholding))
- **assembly-quality-threshold-filtering**: Use when paired-end Illumina FASTQ reads have been assembled (or must be filtered) and you need to set an accept/discard cutoff on the PANDAseq assembly quality score to control sequence recovery, as for the 673 845-read M. capsulatus 16S rRNA library.
